Sultan dogone fari kyakyawa shi ba mai yawan Magana bane shi ba mai son             hayani yabane bai da yawan surutu sai inda babban abokinsane hamza nan zakajishi hira harda dariya sambai sun raini. 




Afnan farace tas mai dirarranjiki tana da sura ta. ban sha'awa ga tsafta da kula dakai 
      Afnan dai kyakyawace ta ajinfarko kuma haka Allah yayi ta da farin jini bata fita nan da nan batare da wani ya biyotaba amma kash bakulasu take ba kasance war ita batashir yayin aure yanzuba.




A bangaren Afnan kuma gidan yaya sadiq tawuce tana zuwa matar Sa halima  tasamu a tsakar gida tana shara saboda yau Yar aikinta ta makara bata zo dawuriba tai sallama ta bude kofa ta shigo halimace ta dago tabar sharar da take don ganin wake sallama koda dagowar ta saitaga afnan 
  Afnan sannu dazuwa shiga ciki ganinan zuwa Afnan tawuce tashiga daki tasamu wuri ta zauna

HALIMA kenan matar YAYA sadiq HALIMA macece mai son abinduniya kuma batadahakuri kuma bata damu da dangin mijinta ba bata cika zuwa in. dasu keba in ko kaga tashiga cikinsu sadiq ne yamatsa mata taje danko shi sadiq hakurin zama yakeyi da ita HALIMA bata taba hai huwaba sheka ransu 2 da aure Allah bai basu hai huwaba.




HALIMA tai sallama ta shigo inda Afnan take Afnan ta gai da anty halima batun sun gama gai sawa ta tanbayi anty halima ko ina yaya sadiq yake sai halima tace yatafi wurin aiki amma ya bani sako na baki halima taciro sakon taba Afnan sukai sallama 


Afnan tafito tashiga motarta ta wuce gida

     Anty Amarya tai caraf ta cafe zancan dace wa kuma bacikar kiyar tar biya gare taba dan inda tana da tarbiya da bata Aikata abinda ta aikataba mts shiyasa tunfarko nace kar'abata mota amma saboda

MAI YASA ANTY AMARYA TAKE KUSHE Afnan  


Alhj Abdullahi da hjy hajara auren soyayya sukayi suna  tsawon shekaru 3 Allah baibasu haihuwaba tun  abunbaida munsu hartakai yafara damun Hajiya hajara wata rana Alh abdullahi yadawo daga kasuwa tasameshi da batun akan yana sonsuje ganin doctor dan tafara zargin ko akwai wata matsalane hakan  kuwa akai sunje kuma doctor yatabbatar musu da ba matsala  lokacine kawai tare da basu  shawara to haka sukaci  gaba da rokon Allah Allah maji rokon bawa sai gashi hjy hajara nada ciki wata  4 murna agun Alh abdullahi ba magana sunreni cikinsu cikin so da kaunar juna har Allah ya sauketa lfy ansha shagali bakadanba yaro yaci sunan  sadiq to adaidai wannan lokacine Allah yakara azurta Alh abdullahi domin kuwa dadukiya namagana to tabbas zatayi bazance tajawo hankalin  mutane kansa  damasu bakin cikin damasu farin  cikin cikin kuwa harda makocinsu wato mahaifin Firdausi yaso ace bazawarar yarsa CE ta samu nasaran auransa wato Firdausi  yayinda itama hakance  azuciyarta akwana atashi ba wuya awanjan Allah gashida yau sadiq yacika shekara biyu aduniya ranar datayi daidai dazuwa sabon alamari ga iyalin Alh abdullahi ne akwance akan gadansa na alfarma yayinda yaji duk duniya babu abinda yakeso sama da auran  Firdausi da sauri yatashi ya na Hajiya Hajiya dagudu tafito daga daki yana irin wan nan kira cewa yayi INA zuwa bari inje daurin aure  na hjy hajara kuma da daddarennan Alhj yace eh kamar wasa yace nawa za'adau ra dariya kawai. yayi YANa me cewa Kedai zuba idoki   sha kallo hajiya nazaune a falo sai taji limamin gidan suna daura auren Alhj abdullahi da amaryarsa fiddausi musa mikewa tsaye tayi tanacewa Oooo ni hajara wannan wana irin tashin hanka line tana me fashe wa da kuka Alhj abdullahi ne yashi go cike da farin ciki yana mai cewa hjy kin ga ikon Allah ko uhmm ta i ya cemishi tabar shi a tsaye tashige daki tana kuka 
 

   

Abin dayasa kukaji andaura auren fiddausi da Alhj a wannan daren kuwa shine fiddausi takai Alhj abdullahi wurin wani boka da kawarta Zaituna taba talabari acikin daren asirin Yakama Alhj abdullahi acikin Daren yaji bawar dayake sosai fiddausi acikin daren yatafi gurin mahai fin fiddausi MALAM musa yaroka adaura musu aure dashi da fiddausi

Haka kuwa akayi acikin daren akadaura aure ana idar da sallar isha'i dan zumudi adaren tatare  kawarta Zaitunace tarakota tareda YAN uwanta mata guda 2


Ko da Alhj mas'ud yasamu labarin auren na Abokin Sa sai da yayi mamaki saboda komai Abokin nasa zaiyi saiya nemi shawarar Sa 


Koda yadubi yanayin Abokin nasa sai yay masa uziri yakuma gane bayin kanshi bane

A wannan daren Hajiya hajara taga ikon Allah tasha ki bakin ciki baka danba









Washe garin ranar Hajiya Aisha da Alhaji mas'ud sukaje gun hjy hajara Alhj mas'ud yayiwa hajara bayanin da zata gane halin da mijin ta yake ciki haka hjy Aisha ma takara yimata nasiha takuma kwantar mata da hankali
 
Haka hjy hajara taciga ba da zaman hakuri yau da dadi gobe ba dadi

To wannan kenan




Mommyn Afnan kuwa datunda aka fara ta kame agun jin kalamen Anty Amarya yasata dagowa da sauri sai kuma  tayi murmushi tace yaran mommy maza kushiga dakin Afnan to kawai sukace suna masu wucewa jiki a salube suna shiga mommy Afnan tacewa momin sultan mushiga cikiko sukuwa su sultan da dad tare da abbansu Afnan kuwa tuni suka fuce waje akabar Anty Amarya sake da baki 



Abangarensu minal kuwa suna shiga suka balle da hira da shewa minal nace wa kai besty kin ganki kuwa sai wani kara kyaukikeyi zarah ta amshe da cewa kunsan wani abukuwa tare sukace sai kinfada kajisu iyayan jin labari to nafasa fada Afnan uwar yan san labari harda roko dan Allah fadamana zarah uhmmm nikam Anty Amarya nabani haushi wlh kiga daga Magana harta da gawa mutane hankali tabe baki Afnan tayi tana mecewa keta dama nikam ko Ajikina dan in wannan matarce bakiga komai bama 
Zarah tab aikuwa da agidan mu take da mun Dade da gyaramata zama suka kwashe da dariya minal ta tsagaita dacewa wai nikam besty a inakika hadu da yaya sultan ko ince aina kika sanshi

Dan Allah Afnan kibarni kwanciya zanyi nacemiki bakomai kinki yarda karya kikeso inyi miki

Yawuce a gurinki nikuma banma saniba balle ince yawuce bazadai ki sanar daniba ko to shikenan bari inkira sha9 kidanku zahra kila ita kifada mata

Yace mata babu inda minal zata minal na nan lpy da itada abinda ke cikin ta

Kukan datakeyi tuni yatafi jin abinda sadiq din yasanar na ita

Dama duk abinda nayi yatashi abanza kenan ba abinda yasameta wallahi bata isaba intasan wata batasan wataba saina barar da cikinnan kota wana irin hali bazan taba bari wani da yafi daga gurin wata baniba cewar halima

Saitin bakinta yasamu yakai mata duka saida bakin ya fashe dukda haka sadiq berabu da itaba saida ya tabbatar jikinta yagaya mata 

Yace halima kin manta cewa akwai Allah baki isa kiyi mata abinda Allah beyi mataba duk irin abinda kika mata tana nan lpy hakan bazesa ki barta ba dana zefito duniya kinaji kina gani 

Wani kuka halima tafashe dashi tana cewa wallahi  na rantse baze fitoba nafada maka indai ina Raye bazefitoba

sannan yasa hannun shi a aljihu yatabo takardar dayasa aciki yana kokarin cirowa

 kanshine yashiga juyawa yaji jikinsa nayi mishi ciwo kafa funsa na rawa jikinsa kamar baya taredashi  yanke jiki yafadi

 cikin muyar kuka tace mijin anty kazo dan Allah yanzu sadiq yafadi

Wallahi anty kiyarda dani ba abinda nayi mishi kizo dasauri zakiga abinda nake fada miki

Suna karasawa nafisa tafito dasauri tashiga gidan dakin halima tashiga gurin sadiq takarasa 

Tana kiran sunan shi sadiq sadiq shiru sadiq be amsaba

Kan halima tayi tashaketa tana cewa kasheshi kikayi fadamin burinki yacika kin kasheshi ko 
Halima mekike nema damu meya sadiq baya yimiki kashe shi dakikayi shine sakaiyar dazaki yi mishi ko 

Ba amsa daga bangaren halima danta shaku idanunta sunfito waje nunfashinta ya fara dauke wa 
 
Daidai lokacin mijin nafisa yashigo dakyar ya kwaci halima a hannun nafisa 

Kabarni inkasheta takashe shifa baya motsi cewar nafisa

Yace kibarta da hakkin dayake kanta musan yanda zamuyi da sadiq

Nafisa taci gaba dacewa yana kwance kekuma kina yawo ko ina baki isaba saikin bishi 

Kibarta nafisa kibarta da hakkin sadiq cewar hajiya hajara

Amma ina nafisa bata sake taba 

Alhaji Abdullahine yace nafisa saketa kinji 

Nafisa tasaki halima

Kasa halima tayi tana maida nunfashi 

Nafisa karki sake irin wannan yanzuda kika shaketa data motu kumafa duk abinda tayi kibarta da Allah cewar hajiya hajara

Mami nifa hankalina baze kwantaba sai naga tabar nan gurin 

Barin nan ya zama dole dan bamu San dame tazoba halima mun gode kina iya tafiya cewar hajiya hajara

Ki tashi kitafi kobaki jiba kije kizauna a gidan kekadai dama haka kikeso daga ke se bakin halinki cewar nafisa

Yimata a hankali nafisa haryanzu bata dawo dai_daiba kin shaketa dayawa kibari inta nutsu saita tafi cewar hajiya hajara

Mami halima zanbari ta nutsu yau mami banda kuna gurin nan da kunji bakin lbr dan wallahi bazan raga mikiba sedai kome zefaru yafaru idan uwarki tatashi tafiya gurin boka takai da sunana

Maganarda nafisa tafada yabawa halima mamaki cikin mamaki tace anty nafisa gurin boka kuma

Eh aikin ji abinda nace sarai ko ance miki bamu San komai ba 

Gaskiya bangane a binda kike nufiba sharri kawai zakiyiwa mahaifiyata cewar halima

Sharri zan muku habawa yarinya kinsan komai kekanki shadace akan abinda na sani nafada miki idan maganinki zeyi aiki akan sadiq ni bazeyi komai a kainaba ki tashi kifita waya sani ko wannan maganar danake dake shima wani maga ninne 

Gaskiya nafisa idan kika sake cewa ina zuwa gurin boka gaskiya bazan yi shiruba irin wannan sharri haka

anfada masu zuwa gurin boka karya nayi kocemiki akayi ban saniba duka abinda kikeyi kallon ki kawai na keyi 

Naji idan muje gurin boka da kudinki muke zuwa kuma ina ruwan ki ya akai kika sani saidai indan kema kina zuwa 

Ni Allah yakiyaye gashi nan kinfada da kanki nayi dana sanin hada jini da mukayi dake nagodewa Allah dabaku samu da tareda sadiq ba dakunsamu dabansan wana iri ze zamaba

Ze zama me albarka ahaka sadiq ya gani yace yana so
Kafin ki shiga kifita ki rabanida shi kin makaro sadiq nawane ni kadai

Karya kikeyi dankuwa sadiq nawarkewa zaki koma gida kicigaba dacin towo 

Kidena wahalar da kanki wajan shaga tsakanina da sadiq dankuwa muriga munzama hanta da jini bama rabuwa

Nikuwa ina miki albi shirin cewa zaku rabu idan kuka rabu nikuma zantuno miki da wannan maganar da kika fada 
Nariga nabaroki halima duk solan jimawa A nan ne tayanda maganinki zeyi yanda kike bukata

Inkina maganar magani dariya kike bani inrasa wanda zankai seke ina kudan bale ro manta
Kibari inyida ishashu bakeva

Mami kinjida kunnenki yatabbata halima nazuwa gurin boka 

Nafisa dan Allah kubar zancen nan kome kayi Allah nagani zesakawa wanda aka zalunta

Ina Alhaji Abdullahi canna ganoshi ya hado tagumi yazubawa sadiq ido bemasan abinda sukeyiba sa boda tsananin tashin han kalinda yake ciki

halima kifita kafin infita dake cewar nafisa

Nifa ba inda zani nice yadace in kulada mijina bakuba kubarni inyi aikina

Wana aiki ok nagane mubarki ki karasa shi ko kifita ko infitar dake yanzu

halima na tsaye batada niyar tafiya

kanta nafisa tayo koda ta tuno irin shakarda tasha saitai saurin fita

Koda dare yayi hajiya hajara tadawo gida tabar Alhaji Abdullahi acan asibiti dashida suleman nafisama takoma gida

hajiya hajara na shigowa

Afnan tace mami waye ba lpy

Kisanar dani wanda ya fada miki inda naje tukunna

Mami anty amaryace kiyarda dani bata cemin komaiba daga nan

Ina minal cewar hajiya

Allah sarki afnan biyoni daki 

to mami

Tana tafe Afnan na binta a baya

Yataso ya zogaban halima yasa hannu ya Ciro takadda ya mika mata

Ni Sadiq Abdullahi nasaki halima Bala saki 3 

Wani kukan kura halima tayi tafisge paper daga hannun nafisa tanacewa bani bace bani yake nufiba ko sadiq fada musu kace musu bani kake nufiba

 dayawan bin bokaye nasan wannan wallahi fiddausi ashirye nake dake duk ta inda kika fito zan iya miki jahila Mara hankali

  Ke ko a mafarki karki kara hada kanki da yanmatan Canada sunfiki komai da komai duk wanda yace miki kinada kyau ya yaudarekine kawai dan ba haka bane kindaiyi sa'a kinsamu hadadan gaye irina kinsan in kika rasani baza kisamu yaniba dan ga alama nafara gani kin tsayardani kina kare mini kallon ya isa haka akwai gobe hmm Afnan kenan nafasan komai

Zainab ki bude kofar dan Allah kefa amanace a gareni tin ranar da kikazo gidan nan kika shiga dakin nan baki fitoba ba abinda kikeyi sai aikin kuka kite maka ki bude ko a bincine kici 

Zainab najinshi tayi shiru sai hawayanda ke kwaranyowa daga fuskarta ya kara ya waita

Zainab kosau1ne kitemaka kibude kiji a binda zan fada miki kinji Zainab kibude 

Cikin muryar kuka Zainab tace dan Allah kabarni katafi Kabarni bazan fitoba 

Zainab yazakice haka yaza ayi nasamu nutsuwa bayan kina kulle a daki Zainab kifito kisanar dani  damuwarki kulle kanki a daki baze taba zama mafitaba 

hakan yayimin katafi kabarni dan Allah cewar Zainab

Zainab kifus kanceni bawai naki zamanki a dakibane a'a kifito kici abinci kidena kuka shine nake bukata Zainab kindagamin hankali kifito dan Allah

Nifa nafada maka bazan fitoba kaki gane wa bazan sake maganaba katafi ko ka tsaya duk daya ne a gurina

To shikenan bari nakira Alhaji Abdullahi nafada mishi tinda ninace ki fito kinki

 Idan yafadawa daddy A kwai matsala dankuwa sai raina yabaci gashi wayata ta mutu balle na kira mommy nafita kawai shine mafita Zainab tafada acikin zuciyar ta

Barinaje nakirashi cewar bala

A'a karka kirashi zanfito cewar Zainab

Alhamdulillah bala yafada a cikin zuciyar shi

Zainab tabude tafito 

Bala yakalleta 

Yace Zainab dan Allah kalli yanda kika koma kinrame kinyi baki Zainab ki kwantar da hankalinki yanzu kije kiyi wanka bari nasiyo miki abinci 

Yana gama fada yafita 

Wanka tayi tashirya tazauna tana jiran dawo warsa 

Bajimawa bala yadawo hannunsa daukeda leda da sunan gurin daya siyo a jiki

Kai tsaye dakinda Zainab take yawuce

Salama yayi tareda bude kofar

Zainab ta amsa 

Zainab ga abinci kici

To tace sannan ta amsa tabude tana ci

Kallonta kawai bala yakeyi yana mamakin ramewarta cikin kwana2

Zainab taci abinci sosai

Mommy ina kike kizo dan Allah mommy bazan iya zama da wannan mutuminba tafada acikin zuciyar ta saikuma tasaki kuka mai ratsa zuciya😭

kingani ko nifa Zainab wannan kukan da kikeyi banaso wallahi dawannan kukan gwara kisanar dani damuwarki

cikin muryar kuka tace damuwata baza tai maka dadin jiba damuwata tawace nikadai nadena yarda da kowa shiyasa nazabi kulle kaina a kan nafito na ganka

Meye dani da bakyaso kiganni cewar bala

Saboda bana sonka bani na zabekaba mahaifinane yazabe ka ni inada wanda nake so tacigabada kuka😭

Shiru yayi yakasa cewa komai 

Nasan maganar dana fada baze maka dadi ba kaika tambayeni shiyasa nafada maka wanda nakeso an bashi Afnan nikuma ya hadani dakai taya zanyi farin cikin

Kokan be batamin raiba Zainab wanda kikeso ba mijinki bane kowana mace da mijinta kamar yanda kowana namiji da matarsa Zainab zakiyi farinciki da zabinda mahaifinki yayi miki

Nidai rokona a gareki shine karki kara kulle kanki a daki saboda wani Abu dabekai ya kawoba dan Allah

Ammafa bana sonka cewar Zainab

Nasani Zainab saidai wanda kikeso besanma kinayi ba yana canda matarsa 

Nasani cewar Zainab

Dan haka kimai da hankalinki ga sauyinda Allah yayi miki

Yana gama fada ya fita

haba malam bazan iya zama dakaiba mommy nake jira tazo tana zuwa zansan abinyi Zainab tafada a fili

Tabude duk abinda ta dafa ya gani yakuma zabi wanda yake so ta zuba mai 

Cikawai yakeyi ba dan yatoshemai yunwa ba dan hanka linshi baya gurin abincin yana gurin Afnan dake gefe sai jefomai murmushi take

Banruwa insha Afnan

Tazuba ta bashi badai ka koshiba naga kana kokarin tashi

Nakoshi Afnan naci sosai Abincin yayi dadi kamar yanda kikayi kyau

Nagode tashi yayi jiki a salube ze shiga dakin shi dasauri tasha gabanshi tana cewa hamza yabada sako a kawo maka mu shiga ka ansa zahra yaba ta kawo min

Au dama yana gurinki tun dazu nake jira shi yakawo min shiga ki miko min cewar sultan

Cikin shagwaba Afnan tace Kashigo dai ka ansa dan bacci nakeji ina shiga sai bacci 

Toshi kenan muje na ansa

Abakin gado ya zauna yana jiranta ta Ciro ta bashi 

Taita lalube jakarta bawai dan bataga abinda take nemaba tagani kawai ra'a yintane Wannan

Taciro tana rangwada harta karaso gabanshi 

Wanda hakan ya gogewa sultan hadda

Gashi 

Aikuwa shine yamike da niyar tafiya yace saida safe

Jintayi shiru batace komai ba yasa yakaraso daf da ita 

Yace bakice komaiba

Tai murmushi tace saida safe kace kuma kasan bashi kadai yadace kace

Ko to shi kenan cewar sultan

hada bakin shi yayi da nata yana kissing dinta harshan shi yazura a bakinta yana yanemo nata harshen yana tsotsa 

Sakar mishi jikinki Afnan tayi yaci gaba da sarrafata hannun shi ya kai kan zip din rigarta ya zuge shi take

 rigar tayi kasa bayyanar dukiyar fulanin ta saida yasa numfashin sultan ya kusa dauke wa ya mika hannun shi

 gurin ya shiga wasa dasu ya dauketa can_cak ya dora ta kan gado yashiga wasa dasu cikin wani irin salo da Afnan ta kasa
gane wa 



            SULTAN  AFNAN ASUBA TA GARI
          ALLAH YA KAWO ZURI A TA GARI

       🤔SU AFNAN AN ZAMA MANYA

Halima dai zama ba dadi tsaka ninta da nasiru wuya ba irin wanda nasiru baya bata amma ta daure ta zauna dashi a haka tafada wa kanta cewa abinda taiwa sadiqne nasiru yake ramawa sadiq


Nasiru nasiru kata shi mana

Kaiiiiiiiiii wai halima bana fada miki idan ina bacci ba a tashina ba

Kafada min ko ince nasani wai rayuwa zata yu a haka kazo ka kwanta kana bacci gida ba komai 

Haka na gada ma kinga halima da banyi roko ba kuma a kanki bazanje inyi roko ba dan haka kifita kibani wuri tin muna kallon juna da mutun ci

Haba nasiru kazabi kabarni da yunwa saboda wani da lili naka katina fa bani na tsara hakan ba Allah yayi bakai zan auraba lokacin amma yanzu ba gani a gidan kaba

Haka ne na yarda amma ke kika tsara wula kantani ko kika cimin mutunci saboda mekudi harda mahaifiyarki a wula

 kantani badan ina ganin mutuncin mahaifinki ba wlh a binda zan miki a gidan nan ko kare baze ciba 

Kai nasiru kaji tsoran Allah  kane momin abinda zanci 

Kince Allah kuma yafi karfin wasa bari na fito kije to 

Zaka fita da gaske 

Ahhh nace zan fita karkisa na fasa wlh

Waiyooo murya nakeji kamar na umma metazo yi to

Ke yanzu a Wannan a kurkin kike zaune tun farko da kinji magana ta da haka be faruba cewar Asabe

Umma dan Allah kiyi shiru yana ciki karya jiki

Yaji mana ina ruwana me matacciyar zuciya yanzu duk irin abinda nayi maka halima taimaka saida ka zagayo kadawo ka aureta dan bakada zuciya

Bakinta halima ta rufe
   Tana cewa umma kirufamin asiri karki ballo min ruwa indan Yaji yanzu a wata matsa lar zaki sake SANI

Ina kake dan mara zuciya kafito da kai nake kanajina 

Nasiru ya Tsugunna a gabanta yana cewa Umma ina kwana 

Rike gaisuwar ka bana bukata zuwa nai na fada maka ka saki halima dan ba a jinka bace Mara zuciya 

Umma indai a kan halima ne ki kirani kome kika gadama kuma batun saki ni bazan saketa ba cewar nasiru

Nasani dan gidan marasa zuciya wanda kare ya lashe base kayi hakaba zansan kabirune ya haifeka ba kokaki kokaso yau zata bar gidanka

Umma ban yarda ki fitar min da mata taba kuma ni na yarda banida zuciya indai a kan halima ne ina son mata ta nafita saina dawo umma ki gaida gida

Yafita yabarsu

 Wani farin cikine ya ziyarci zuciyar halima jin maganar da nasiru yafada hawa yan farin cikine ya zubo a fuskar ta 

Ina ruwanka da gidana  Nina rasa irin Wannan naci irin na nasiru ke halima ki karewa kanki kallo kiga yanda kikayi baki kika rame 

Murmushi kawai halima tayi tace Umma yasu baba 

yana gurin Amaryar sa yau zanyita ta kare tsaka Nina da Amaryar babanki kizo muje ko kema a kwai wani te makonda boka ze miki

😳waaaaaniiiiii a'a umma sekin dawo kullum addu'a na shine nasiru ya gane gaski yau a nan a idona yace yana sona Wannan ma ya isheni umma 

Ke waya gaya miki na miji nada tabbas ya fadane kawai dan yanuna min cewa yana sonki Amma a zuciyar shi ba haka bane bari na fada miki kisan a binyi tin wuri wlh

Nidai ina kaiwa Allah kukana kuma na fara ganin canji sosai dan haka umma nagode da kulawarki a gareni ba gurin wanda zanje kema addu'a nake miki Allah yaganar dake

Haka kika ce to shi kenan da kanki zakizo kice na rakaki kuma lokaci ya kure ba inda zanje kina jiko

Natafi 

To umma kigaida gida 

Bata am saba tayi waje 

Wani karamin yaro ya shigo Salama Alaikum nanne gidan halima

Eh nan ne

   Waini Asabe yanzu ina zani ina zandosa yauwa ninasan gurin zuwa Bari naje gurin boka na bayan gari shiya San kankomai zekuma kaini inda ya dace

Ehhh to bari na duba na gani

 zainab suna zaune lpy kuma duk abinda zeyi baze same suba hasa lima kanshi ze dawo

waya fada miki hasalima ni badake nake maga naba daga yanzu harna gama abinda zanyi karki sake magana koda nina yi magana kina jiko

To boka nayi shiru cewar Asabe

Nanfa boka ya sake surutai ruwa ya canza yanuno mishi gidan halima 

Halima yaganin cikin farin ciki nanma yaga ba abinda ze iya yi akai 

Aiki Ja komai ya da gule Babu abinyi idan na sanar da ita zuciyarta zata fashe ta fadi ta mutu anan nikuma a kamani da laifin kisa bari na gano bala ko zan samu nasara a kanshi

 boka ya daga ido ya kalli Asabe yasun kuyar dakai tareda gir_giza kanshi

Asabe ido yayi Ja yafito waje duk motsin da boka yayi akan idonta har leka ruwan kwaryar takeyi zufa na yan kowa daga kanta zuwa fusfuskarta


Kwaryar ya nuno mishi bala nan ma yaga ba abinyi 

Yayi shiru yana kallo Asabe

 yace gaskiya kinyi mugun sa'a inba niba bawanda ze iya Wannan aikin yanzudai zan baki Wannan maga nin kije

 gidan ki zuba a bakin kofa zakiga abin mama ki ita kuma halima Kibar min komai a hannuna Wannan aikin nawane

 bala kuwa a nan nagama maganin Sa inkinje zaki gani abin ba a cewa komai

Naji dadi maganar nan Wannan shine kudin aikinka Wannan kuma kyauta na baka sainazo da da maka wasu idan aiki yayi kyau cewar Asabe

Godiya nake yi cewar boka

Nice da godiya ba kaiba natafi

Nanfa ta tafi tana sauri harta tari mashin ta hau


fiddausi ce a falo tanata kai  kawo fuskar ta cike da damuwa ta zauna ta tashi tana tuna nin a bin yi

Ba abinda ke damuna kamar cikinda ke jikin Zaituna ba abinda yakeyi sai girma ina ji ina gani dole nayi wani abu akai

 inzubar da cikin idan be zube ba sai naje inda ya dace yau zan kawo karshan ki Zaituna

Afusace ta nufi dakinda Zaituna take ta shiga bugawa da karfi 

Zaituna ta taso ta bude mata kofa tana Bude wa fiddausi tasa hannun ta2 ta dunkudata dim Zaituna ta zube a kasa 

Waiyoo fiddausi ina kike kallo kika tun kudani nafadi  kuma kinsan bani kadai bace cewar Zaituna

Sanin hakanne yasa na tun Ku daki kika fadi kullum da bakin ciki nake kwana nake tashi duk saboda cikin nan yau nayanke shawarar kasheki da abin cikin ki kona huta cewar fiddausi

Kisafa kika ce fiddausi dani da abinda ke cikina munfi kar finki ba abinda kika isa kiyi mana mun zama ciwan ido cewar Zaituna

Hahhhhh dawuri haka kibari kiga nafita na barki cikin ki na jikinki kokuma ke kina raye kafin kice haka

sanin ba abinda kika isa kiyimin shiya bani karfin gyaiwar fadar haka

Nagani Sa rai da kin dauka wasa nakeyi yanzu kuma kintab batarwa kanki cewa da gaske nake cewar fiddausi

Tahowa take izuwa gaban Zaituna 
                 Zaituna kuma baya ta keje dankuwa yanzu tasan fiddausi dagaske takeyi zata iya aikata abinda tace

Kitsaya nayi abinda ya kawoni cikin sauki batareda mun batawa juna lokaciba Zaituna cewar fiddausi

Fiddausi ta sunkuya Ta kamo  kafar Zaituna tana dariya tajan yota ta dunkule hannunta ta daga sama tadawo dashi

 Zaituna na ganin haka ta kifa naushin ya sauka a bayanta saboda a zabar naushin tasa kafa ta tun kude fiddausi ta fadi kasa

Dasauri ta mike tsaye tana rike bayanta tana nishi fiddausi na yun kurin tashi Zaituna ta kaimata duka a baya fiddausi takoma kasa ba shiri 

Dama na fada miki fiddausi Allah baze taba baki sa'a a kainaba wlh

Cikin jinzafin maganar Zaituna fiddausi ta mike cikin zafin nama ta riko kan Zaituna ta buga a bango Zaituna ta saki wani mawuyacin kara harsai da Alhaji da hajiya suka jiyo karar ta

Lpy kuwa Wannan irin kara cewar Alhaji

Gaskiya kam taso mu tafi

Koda fiddausi taga jinin dadi taji takara yowa kan Zaituna

Koda fiddausi taga jini nazubowa daga bakinta takara fusata ranta yabaci sosai tayiwani kukan kura ta mike ta damko gashin kan Zaituna

Ta tunkudata danta hade da bango

Alhaji ido ya Kada yayi Ja bakin shi na rawa hakama hannun shi ya ajiye Zaituna ya karasa gorin fiddausi

fiddausi naniyar magana tsabar haushinta da yake ji yasa shi rufeta da mahaujacin duka 

Abin mamaki shine kokuka batayiba hasalima dariya takeyi tana farin cikin halinda Zaituna take ciki

Alhaji dan Allah kabarta kazo mukai Zaituna asibiti tazubar da jini 

Bahaka nasoba naso ace cikin nan ya bare Wannan naso amma bakomai akwai wata hanyar koda kun kaita asibiti zanzo can na sameta 

Alhaji Abdullahi ya dunkulle hannun yakaimata a fasha_shan bakin ta wanda yasata sakin kara

Tana kallo Zaituna tana murmushin keta 
 
 dagudu yadauki Zaituna suka nufi asibiti nanfa aka yimata taimakon gaggawa ganin ta suma Alhaji da hajiya hanka linsu a tashe

 takeyi bata gan shiba nanta sake sakin karfinta gaba daya wajan hakowa saida tahaka me zurfi tukunna ta ga abinda take

 ne ma yana nan yanda yake kasa kawai ya shige bayan haka beyi komai ba murmushi ya bai baye fiddausi 

Tadaga abin sama tana cewa yau zan kawo karshanki Zaituna,Alhaji, hajiya, Asabe da bala yau zaku gane kuranku da gudu ta fita ta tari mashin ta hau

Likitoci sunjima a gurin Zaituna sannan 1 daga cikin su ya fito

Kamar yanda gidan bokan yake haka tasa meshi da duhu sosai Wannan yafi wanda sukazo tareda Asabe duhu haka ta cusa kai izuwa ciki

Kwance take tana bacci fuskar ta a kunbure goshinta yasha nadi da bandeji 

hajiya kinga aikin fiddausi ko kinga abinda taiwa Zaituna da idonki nace zan sauwake mata kince a'a yanzu idan nayi aiba laifi cewar Alhaji Abdullahi

A'a Alhaji kayi hakuri babu abinda ke dawai niya da fiddausi irin kishi kaci gaba da hakurin da kake yi watarana zata gane gaskiya batun saki kuma ka barshi

Nasani nasan abinda zakice kenan amma duk abinda tayiwa Zaituna taci bulus kenan aishi kenan

Ya maida kallonsa zuwaga Zaituna dake bacci

Koda fiddausi ta gama hutawa ta tashi ta tari adaidaita sahu tahau tafada mishi ida ze kaita 

Baka jiba ko a nan kusa za a samu kogi

Eh to hajiya baza a rasaba nataba zuwa wani kogi gaba da nan kadan a nan yake 

Murmushin keta fiddausi tayi tace ko zaka temaka ka kaini zan iya biya konawa kace 

To hajiya barina kai ki tinda zaki biya 

Nantake ya kara gudun adaidaita sahun yajuya kan adaidaita sahun izuwa inda kogin yake cikin kankanin lokaci suka karasa gurin

Hajiya gamu muzo ki sauko ki gani idan nashiga da adaidaita bazan iya fitowaba saidai in kinaso ki gani da kyau ki karasa da kafa

Toba komai saina karasa din kajirani ina zuwa 

Tafiya take cikin farin ciki ta karasa dafda kogin ta waiga ta kalli me adaidaita sahun hankalin shi sam baya gurin ta

 taji dadin hakan taciro abin ta jefa cikin ruwan tana dariyar mugun ta tajuya ta tafi 
 
Mutafi ko cewar fiddausi

To hajiya har kingajida kallon ruwan yanada girma sosai gashi ba irin kifinda babu da da safe kika zo zakiga gurin yacika da masu kamun kifi

Naga wani ma yana kamawa yan zu ruwan nada girma sosai yanada kyan gani gurin ya birgeni cewar fiddausi

Nasiru yakulada kallon da halima take mishi

Yace halima lpy kuwa 

Ajiyar zuciya tayi tace nasiru da gaske kana sona 

Kallonta yayi tareda yin murmushi cikin tausa sashiyar murya yake

 cewa halima ina qaunar ki harcikin zuciyata *HADARIN SO*    ki haryanzu yana hadowa a cikin zuciyata duk abinda

 namiki shine dan na rama abinda kikayi min abaya yanzu komai ya wuce wato jiya danaje masallaci bayan an idar da

 sallah malam yayi wa'azi yanda kika San yasan abinda ke faruwa a gidana wa'azin ya shigeni sosai insha Allahu

 bazan karaba na jima ina nazarin maganar malam saiga umma tace na sake ki nikadai nasan halinda na shiga komai yawuce halima insha Allahu zakiji dadi kasan cewa a gidana
 
Hawayan farin cikine ke fitowa daga fuskar halima Allah nagode maka cewar halima

Kidena kuka halima ko nima kukan kikeso nayi

Nadena nima bansan lokacinda ya fito tayi murmushi 

Yauwa kokefa nafiso nadinga ganin farin ciki a fuskar ki halima cewar nasiru

Washe gari fiddausi tayi samma kon tashi tayi girki da kanta tazuba ta ajiye tawuce dakin Zaituna

Tabude tashiga ta samu tana bacci ta tsaya akanta tana murmushi tajuya ta fita ta koma kitchen 

Anya zanyi nasara kuwa bacci takeyi naso ace idonta biyu nabata taci a wuce gurin inan inajiranki harki tashi saikinci kinmutu ayau din nan

Taita zuwa tana dubawa saita samu tana bacci tayi hakan kusan sau biyar ana shidan tasamu ta tashi fiddausi taji dadin hakan 

Tace barka da safiya tin dazu nazo dan nadubaki saina samu baki tashi ba bari na kawo miki abinci kici tabude kofa ta fita

Duk abinda fiddausi takeyi Zaituna kallon ta kawai ta keyi 

fiddausi na sanki sarai da akwai abinda kika shirya nafikar finki fiddausi nangani nanbari kome zaki kawo ki kawo ina jiranki cewar Zaituna

Cikin farin ciki da dariyar mugunta ta Bude abincin ta takunce abinda boka yabata tarufe idon ta tazuba akan
abincin tala lubo murfin ta rufe

 tanufi dakin Zaituna ta ajiye Tajuya wa kwanon baya tace gashi kici yanzu ina zuwa inkin gama zanzo na dauke kwanon

Tafita bareda taji amsarda Zaituna zata bata ba 

Kokallo abincin zaituna bata yiba 

hajiya hajara takawo mata abinci taci ta koshi 

fiddausi nacan tana zuba kunne ko zataji an fasa kukan mutuwa taji shiru hanka linta yatashi 

Idan naje zaituna zata zargi wani Abu bari nadan bata lokaci

 yunwa fiddausi take ji amma taki cin komai burinta ace Zaituna bata duniyar lokacin zataci abinci

Yunwa sai nukurkusarta yakeyi gashi haryanzu bataji ihoba 

Zakariya dake gefenta ta kalla tace zakariya kaje kasamu Rabi kace tabaka abinci kakawo min inci yunwa nakeji

To mommy yace yafita yana wasan shi yana kaiwa kofar dakin zaituna ya tsaya cak

Yau anty Zaituna bata fitoba bari nadubata yace tareda bude kofar dakin ya shiga

Anty Zaituna inakwana cewar zakariya

Lpy kalau ya kake 

Lpy anty Zaituna shine kika zauna adaki kika ki fitowa ko

Yanzu nake shirin fitowa zakariya

Anty Zaituna yaciwanki ya warke 

Eh yawarke 

Barinaje kitchen ina zuwa anty 

Zakariya dauki abincin nan ka tafi dashi  kitchen

To anty Zaituna yana dauka Yaji nauyi 

Yace anty Zaituna yanaji da nauyi bakici bane saina gayawa daddy bakyason cin abinci

Naci wan can zakariya duba kagani nakoshine shiyasa banci Wannan ba cewar Zaituna

To natafi yabude kofa yafita

Cakya tsaya yace mommy tace na anso mata abinci ga Wannan kuma anty Zaituna bata Ciba malamin mu yace bakyau wula kanta abinci bari na kai mata shi kawai 

Ya nufi dakin fiddausi da abincin

Asabe tashirya tafito daga gidanta tatari mota ta hau 
Akofar gidan bala aka ajiyeta taciri kudinshi ta bashi 

Yatafi taci sa'a gidan a Bude tadura tashiga takarasa bakin kofar shiga gidan takunce gullin zani taciro garin maganin ta kunce daurin ta zuba a kofar shiga cikin gidan koda taji alamar fitowa tayi saurin juyawa da niyar barin gidan

Zakariya yabude kofa yashiga mommy ga abincin kici 

Yauwa zakariya Allah yamaka Albarka

Tabude  tafara ci

 kai abincin nan yayi dadi zakariya kai gaba dayama zan cinye

Ko mommy nasan zeyi dadi shiyasa nakawo miki

Gyada kaitakeyi alamar abincin yamata dadi sai saba loma take yi
       Baki a cike da abinci take cewa zakariya waya yi girkin nan medadi

  Abinda tazuba ya canza kallar abincin wanda ita kanta bata saniba saka makon idonta a rufe

Dariya yayi yace bansa niba nima adakin anty Zaituna yake tace nakai kitchen nikuma na kawo miki cewar zakariya

furzarda abincin bakinta tayi gaba daya tasaki ihu waiyoo nashiga 3 zakariya ka gama dani dakyar takarasa magana

Cikinta yashiga murdawa idonta namata wani iri sai sosa idon takeyi tana rike ciki tamin mike a tsakiyar dakin sai

 birgima fiddausi takeyi bakinta yashiga zubarda jini tin tana iya ganin zakariya harta dena ganin shi 

Zakariya yafita da gudu ya kira hajiya hajara,Zaituna, da Alhaji Abdullahi

Dagudu suka shiga dakin Halin da suka tsinci fiddausi aciki ya gigitasu yakuma basu tausayi ganin jini da wani irin Abu NA fitowa daga bakinta

Koda Asabe taga ankusa bude kofa saita kara sauri amma ina bala yabude yakuma gane ta 

Dakarfi yace Asabe 

Tana jin haka tasaki karfinta gaba 1 tana gudu yana binta a baya harta fito bakin titi 

Asabe kitsaya nine bala Asabe ki Tsaya mana jikake gam Asabe tazube kasa

Kudu take bata kallon gabanta harta iso titi mota tataho da gudu yabigeta tafadi yabi takan kafarta ya yuce

Innalillahiwainnailaihir rajiun shine abinda bala ya iya furtawa

Dasauri yakaraso gurinta kankace me mutane sunzagayesu suna kallo

Mota ya tsare yasa Asabe aciki suka wuce asibiti

Zainab taga abinda yafaru dama ta iya mota saita dauki mukulli tabi bayansu

Alhaji Abdullahi ya dauki fiddausi yasa a mota Zaituna da hajiya hajara suka shiga mota yaja suka tafi

Atare motar Alhaji Abdullahi dana bala suka shigo zainab tashigo daga baya bala yaciro Asabe yashiga da ita Alhaji Abdullahi ma ciki yashigada fiddausi

Basu ankara da zainab ba itakuma tinda tashigo suta fara gani gabansu tazo ta Tsaya 

Tagaida hajiya hajara da Zaituna suka ansa Zaituna da hajiya hajara kallon kallo sukeyi sunkasa cewa zainab komai 

Zaituna ce  tayi karfin halin cewa me kika zoyi zainab

Anty  Zaituna kinga Asabe ce tazo tsallaka titi mota ya bigeta 

Allah sarki sukace gaba dayansu

Anty Kukuma wa kuka kawo anty

Ehhh😳zaituna ta kalli hajiya 

Kinga zainab samu guri ki zauna babu Wannan lokaci mudai Allah ya bata lpy cewar hajiya hajara

Ameeen zainab tace 

Likitoci sunyi iya yinsu saidai cutar fiddausi yafi karfinsu tana nan yanda aka kawo ta 

Alhaji Abdullahi gaskiya mun kasa gano ma tsalar matarka kushiga kuganta abingaba yakeyi kuma cewar likita

Bakomai bari naje gurinta yana shiga Zaituna zainab hajiya suka rufa mishi baya 

 mommy meya same ki cewar zainab

Waya kawo kinan zainab ina mijin ki cewar Alhaji Abdullahi

Bamuda Wannan lokacin yanzu meye abinyi yanzu cewar Zaituna

Babu dan sunce bama suga ne aina hin cutar dake damunta  ba cewar Alhaji

Haka suka sata gaba suna kallo 

Bari naje waje ina zuwa cewar Alhaji Abdullahi

Yana fita ya haduda malam Ibrahim

Sukayi musa biha 

Malam lpy na ganka a nan cewar Alhaji Abdullahi

Nazo ganin likitane kaifa cewar malam Ibrahim

fiddausi ce balafiya ata kaice madai likitoci basusan abinda yake damuntaba tana ciki

Ah muje na dubata

To mushiga

Fiddausi sai doke doke takeyi tamirgino ta fado daga kan Gado

Yashigo suka gaisa da matan Alhaji zainab kasa cewa komai tayi aikin kuka kwai takeyi

Malam Ibrahim yakarewa fiddausi kallo yace Abdullahi samo min ruwa 

To malam yana fita ya shigo da ruwa a hannun shi ya yamika wa malam

Malam Ibrahim yayi tofi yaba hajiya yace gashi ki bata abaki ki wanke mata fuska dashi

To tace tayi kamar yanda ya sata 

Ruwan na shiga bakin fiddausi tafara amai me wari tana fidda wani dunkulen Abu tabaki tajima tana aman haka suka jure suka zauna

Saikuma ta dena aman tagala baita a gurin ta kwanta tana yunkurin bude ido

Tana Bude wa tayi ido2 da Zaituna saita fashe da kuka ta mika wa Zaituna hannu Zaituna tarike hannu fiddausi tana kuka tarungu meta bataji kyankya minta ba 

Duk kansu sunji tausayin fiddausi 

Malam yace Abdullahi yi mata magana 

To malam fiddausi 

Na'am kuyafeni kuyafe min 

Alhamdulillah cewar malam

Nan fiddausi tasanar dasu shirinta akan Zaituna 

Malam yayi mata wa'azi taita kuka 

Saidai fiddausi bata gani sosai 

Kutashi mutafi gida cewar Alhaji Abdullahi

Malam yace ni zan tafi Allah yakara sauki

Ameeen suka fito gaba dayansu hajiya hajara tarike fiddausi sai aikin kuka takeyi

Bala suka gani abisa dukkan alamu baya cikin hanka linshi

Alhaji Abdullahi yace bala lpy

Yace  Asabe za'a yankewa kafa sun shiga da ita 

Duk suka shiga jajanta mishi 

fiddausi kuka ta kuma fashewa dashi 

Hakika Wannan Abu ya isheni ishara ban isa inyi abinda Allah beyiba dana mutu mezan cewa Allah fiddausi tafada a zuciyarta

Malam yatafi sukuma suka zauna suna jiran fitowar Asabe

Baji mawa aka fitoda Asabe sukayi kanta aka shigada Asabe daki 

Sukayiwa bala sallama suka koma gida

Suna shiga gida fiddausi ta fashe da kuka 

Zaituna hajiya Alhaji kuyafemin duk abinda na muku bazan karaba nayi nadama

Munyafe miki Allah ya yafe mana suka hada baki sukace 

Kitafi dakin ki fiddausi kije ki kwanta cewar hajiya

To hajiya tace tamike daniyar tafiya tafadi kasa 

Dasauri hajiya ta dagota tace lpy fiddausi 

hajiya idona bana gani sosai komai yayimin duhu cewar fiddausi

Bakomai zansan abinyi cewar Alhaji Abdullahi

Hajiya takaita daki

nagode sosai Allah yakara budi cewar Afnan
